# Source reference and withholding ledger

Source-relative paths in preserved reports/CSV are **artifact IDs from the original local audit**. A withheld file is a publication choice, not evidence that the paper or repository lacks that artifact. No raw third-party paper/source/page, dataset, model weight, or original Git history is included. Use official links and source hashes below.

See [machine-readable index](source_reference_index.csv) and [full export manifest](archive_manifest.csv). The original local audit is retained unchanged at source commit `47956bf6c3eaa0cceee3ac5e525432ca6919b478`.
